  <div data-component-id="campus:backgroundImageHeader" class="container display-heading inverse-color">
    <h1>
      Re&shy;search
    </h1>
  </div>

  <div data-component-id="campus:backgroundImageHeader" class="nodeHeader__grid-container container">

    <div data-component-id="campus:backgroundImageHeader" class="first-col grid-half display-heading inverse-color">
              <section class="manchet" aria-label="Short introduction|t">
         <p class="c-manchet-1">            Copenhagen Business School (CBS) is an internationally recognised business university with deep roots in the Nordic socio-economic model. With a broad social science profile, CBS conducts research at the intersection of business, society and leadership – with a strong focus on disciplinary depth and interdisciplinary insight.
      </p>
        </section>
          </div>
    <div class="second-col grid-half">
              <div class="cta-buttons">
                  </div>
      
      
    </div>
  </div>
</header>





      <div class="container">
  <div data-drupal-messages-fallback class="hidden"></div>

</div>

<article  data-component-id="campus:twoColumnText" class="component container two-col-text-component component--view-mode--default">
  <div  data-component-id="campus:twoColumnText" class="paper-page">

                <h2 id="25632-research-with-societal-impact" class="two-col-header pt-none c-title-2 sb mb">            Re&shy;search with So&shy;ci&shy;et&shy;al Im&shy;pact
      </h2>
    
        
<div  class="text_wrapper row row-auto row-gap-xlarge">
                <div class="body-headings"><p>CBS addresses some of today’s most pressing societal challenges, including the green transition, responsible leadership, digitalisation, artificial intelligence and the future of work. Our strategy “We transform society with business” commits us to turning knowledge into action – in close collaboration with businesses, public authorities and civil society.</p>
<p>&nbsp;</p>
<h3>&nbsp;</h3>
<p>&nbsp;</p>
</div>
          <div class="body-headings"><p>Research at CBS is organised across 11 departments and a number of research centres and cross-disciplinary initiatives. Together, they represent a significant share of Denmark’s leading research in fields such as sustainable leadership, finance, innovation, entrepreneurship, digital technologies, organisational studies and corporate governance.</p>
<p><a href="/en/research/research-profile" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="bac612fa-6300-4aba-b898-d1665fe929b7" data-entity-substitution="canonical">See our Research Profile</a></p>
</div>
      
  </div>
  </div>
</article>

                    PhD sup&shy;port
      
      </h2>
        
      </div>


  <div class="paper-page">
    <div class="content_wrapper row row-no-gap colorprofile flip-grid dark-variant" id="25659-phd-support">
      <div class="content display-heading  color-primary-corporate-white" id="25659-phd-support">
                <div class="content_text">
          <p class="c-manchet-1">            The CBS PhD Support manages the administration of PhD students in the PhD study at CBS. We provide counselling and guidance of current and future PhD students, supervisors, PhD coordinators, heads of department, heads of secretariat and external partners in all administrative matters relating to the PhD programme at CBS.
      </p>
                      

  <a href="/en/phd"   data-component-id="campus:button" class="btn btn-hover-scroll color-text-black">

      <span class="visually-hidden"></span>
    </span>

<article data-component-id="campus:mediaText" class="component media-text-component container colorprofile-component component--view-mode--default global-">

  

  <div data-component-id="campus:componentHeading" class="heading_parent">
                              <h2  data-component-id="campus:componentHeading" class="c-title-1 sb pt-none" id="29592-scancor-at-cbs">
                    SCAN&shy;COR at CBS
      
      </h2>
        
      </div>


  <div class="paper-page">
    <div class="content_wrapper row row-no-gap colorprofile dark-variant" id="29592-scancor-at-cbs">
      <div class="content display-heading  color-primary-corporate-white" id="29592-scancor-at-cbs">
                  <div data-component-id="campus:componentHeading" class="color-primary-corporate-white" id="29592-scancor-at-cbs">
                          <h3 class="inherit-text-color a-display-3">            Ad&shy;vance your re&shy;search in&shy;ter&shy;na&shy;tion&shy;ally
      </h3>
                      </div>
                <div class="content_text">
          <p class="c-manchet-1">            SCANCOR offers CBS scholars the chance to spend time at Stanford or Harvard and join a global community working on organizations and leadership. SCANCOR-Scandinavia provides additional opportunities for focused research stays and international academic collaboration.
      </p>
                      

  <a href="/en/research/scancor-scandinavian-consortium-organizational-research"   data-component-id="campus:button" class="btn btn-hover-scroll color-text-black">

        Discover SCANCOR opportunities
      </span>
      <span class="visually-hidden"></span>
    </span>

  <div data-component-id="campus:backgroundImageHeader" class="container display-heading inverse-color">
    <h1>
      Forsk&shy;ning
    </h1>
  </div>

  <div data-component-id="campus:backgroundImageHeader" class="nodeHeader__grid-container container">

    <div data-component-id="campus:backgroundImageHeader" class="first-col grid-half display-heading inverse-color">
              <section class="manchet" aria-label="Short introduction|t">
         <p class="c-manchet-1">            Copenhagen Business School (CBS) er en internationalt anerkendt business school med stærke rødder i den nordiske samfundsmodel. CBS har en bred samfundsvidenskabelig profil og bedriver forskning i krydsfeltet mellem erhverv, samfund og ledelse – med fokus på både disciplinær dybde og tværfaglig indsigt.
      </p>
        </section>
          </div>
    <div class="second-col grid-half">
              <div class="cta-buttons">
                  </div>
      
      
    </div>
  </div>
</header>





      <div class="container">
  <div data-drupal-messages-fallback class="hidden"></div>

</div>
  
        <div class="components_spacing">            


  
<article  data-component-id="campus:twoColumnText" class="component container two-col-text-component component--view-mode--default">
  <div  data-component-id="campus:twoColumnText" class="paper-page">

                <h2 id="20180-forskning-med-samfundsmoessig-gennemslagskraft" class="two-col-header pt-none c-title-2 sb mb">            Forsk&shy;ning med sam&shy;funds&shy;mæs&shy;sig gen&shy;nem&shy;slags&shy;kraft
      </h2>
    
        
<div  class="text_wrapper row row-auto row-gap-xlarge">
                <div class="body-headings"><p>CBS beskæftiger sig med nogle af tidens mest presserende samfundsudfordringer – herunder grøn omstilling, ansvarligt lederskab, digitalisering, kunstig intelligens og fremtidens arbejdsmarked. vores strategi "We transform society with business" forpligter os til at skabe viden, der gør en forskel – i tæt samarbejde med virksomheder, myndigheder og civilsamfundet.&nbsp;</p>
<p>&nbsp;</p>
<p>&nbsp;</p>
</div>
          <div class="body-headings"><p>CBS’ forskning er organiseret i 11 institutter og en række forskningscentre og -miljøer. Herfra udgår en stor andel af Danmarks førende forskning inden for områder som bæredygtig ledelse, finansiering, innovation, entreprenørskab, organisation, digital teknologi og corporate governance.&nbsp;</p>
<p><a href="/forskning/forskningsprofil" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="bac612fa-6300-4aba-b898-d1665fe929b7" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Se vores forskningsprofil</a></p>
</div>
      
  </div>
  </div>
</article>

          <p class="c-manchet-1">            Ph.d.-skolen tilbyder et internationalt forskningsmiljø af høj kvalitet, hvor ph.d.-studerende udvikler sig som selvstændige forskere.       </p>
